Platts Oilgram Price Report  October 22, 2026

    Platts, part of S&P Global Energy, assessed the US Gulf Coast-
UK/continent Aframax freight rate at $135.51/metric ton Oct. 21,
down $1.06 from the previous session.

    Market activity among native North Sea grades was heard to
be thin, with one North Sea crude trader noting that there were
no Forties offers heard in the market during the session.

    The majority of second-half-November-loading Forties
cargoes were heard to be unplaced earlier in the week.

    Differentials in the regional sour segment continued to decline
Oct. 22 as the ongoing supply glut in the European sour market
dragged on prices.

    A Johan Sverdrup cargo loading out of Mongstad Nov. 4-6
was offered by bp at a premium of $2.85/b to Dated brent. The
offer demonstrated weaker value day over day and was left
outstanding at the London close.

    Apart from inflows of Middle Eastern sour barrels, competing
grades from across the Atlantic have been limiting any potential
upside for North Sea sour barrels.

    "Hearing some Brazilian cargoes are hitting homes in Europe,"
a sour crude trader based in Europe said.

                                                                       -- Ernest Puey

Americas Crude

US oil rig count falls as fracking market remains tight

Permian rig count drops 6, down 12 yearly

Haynesville adds 5 rigs, leads basin gains

Frac crew market tightens with full calendars

    The US oil and natural gas rig count fell by four in the week
to Oct. 21, with oil-focused rigs retreating even as gas activity
increased, and the broader count remained about 23 above
year-ago levels, according to an S&P Global Energy CERA analysis
published Oct. 22.

    The Permian lost six rigs, leaving 239 active and flipping the
annual trend, with the rig count down 12 compared to this time
last year.

    The Eagle Ford Basin was unchanged at 56 active rigs, up
seven year over year, while the Bakken gained one to 37 active
rigs, up five year over year.

    The Denver-Julesburg Basin was up two week over week to 12
active rigs, unchanged year over year. Oklahoma's SCOOP/STACK
play lost one, leaving 26, up just two year over year. Other basins,
mostly oil-focused, lost four, leaving 144, but up five year over year.

    Earlier this month, Crescent Energy purchased Devon Energy's
Eagle Ford assets in a major $3.85 billion dollar deal on Oct. 8.
Targeted to close this quarter or by early 2027, the acquisition
includes some 68,000 barrels/day of oil equivalent of output --
around 40,000 b/d of net crude oil.

    In its announcement, Crescent COO Jerome Hall said that
Karnes Trough is among the lowest break-even oil resources in
the Lower 48. Devon's recent wells demonstrate the potential of

Hectoliter Pure Alcohol (HPA)                                                                                                   Platts FOLicht Ethanol
· Type: Volume Unit
· Definition: A unit of volume that measures the volume of pure                         BFF PGEEU00 lhc  2     QR    EUR HLT Industrial 99 Grade DAP East
(100%) alcohol. One HLTPA equals 1 hectoliter (100 liters) of pure
alcohol. Unlike a standard hectoliter (hL), which measures the                                                                  Europe Eur/HLT Contract Qtly
total volume of any liquid, HLTPA refers only to the pure alcohol
content, regardless of the concentration in the original beverage.                                                              Platts FOLicht Ethanol
